# Twenty additional frontier results — 2026-10-14 campaign

Status: active; target raised by the user on 2026-10-17. **19/20 newly accepted results; one remains.** [R041](findings/R041-four-polytope-six-facet-projection.md) establishes the reverse projection-body volume inequality for four-polytopes with at most six actual facets, with equality exactly for simplices. Proof, substantial-contribution, scoped formal-support, final manuscript and independently verified local release gates passed. The unchanged 20-page paper and portable release are complete; the full geometric theorem is not formalized.

Current instruction (2026-10-17): **stop uploads and focus on20 accepted results**. Further Zenodo/MathDB submissions and browser work are stopped. All mathematical, independent-review, scoped-formal, manuscript and local release gates remain required. R023–R030 remain published; R031–R041 remain accepted and local. See [scope amendment](campaigns/2026-10-14/expanded-target-2026-10-17/research-only-amendment.json).

Structural r133's [T106O133 archive](campaigns/2026-10-14/discovery-structural-r133-parent/assessment.md) has full independent correctness PASS with no repairs and contribution TECHNICAL at the unchanged scope. The major graph-colouring consequence is judged a routine source-proof parameter transfer with classical circular colouring and sandwich/fibre counting. It earns no acceptance or count, and no formal/manuscript/release stage opens. Primary/priority limits remain explicit. Correctness and contribution are separate gates.

Structural r135's T165P135 has a [full-scope contribution TECHNICAL disposition](campaigns/2026-10-14/discovery-structural-r135-parent/contribution-r2-disposition.md), conditional on correctness. Codex round2 addresses all O1–O7: the source's individual shift, integral chord identity and existing diagonal detector compose routinely. The historical Factory SUBSTANTIAL verdict remains preserved but was not adopted. Correctness is unreviewed; no acceptance/count or downstream stage opens. Distinct Codex Astra structural r136 is dispatched.

Structural r134's T184I134 now has a [contribution TECHNICAL disposition](campaigns/2026-10-14/discovery-structural-r134-parent/disposition.md), conditional on correctness, for the full unchanged mean-field/degree-sequence independent-set count. The decisive counting operation already appears in a prior working paper; test weights and Jensen supply the remaining corollaries. Correctness remains explicitly unreviewed. No acceptance, count or downstream formal/manuscript/release stage opens; distinct structural r135 is dispatched.

[T164F99](campaigns/2026-10-14/discovery-arithmetic-r99-parent/disposition.md), finite sums/products in infinite fields, has contribution TECHNICAL conditional on correctness and earns no count; correctness remains unreviewed. [T088P133 is accepted as R041](findings/R041-four-polytope-six-facet-projection.md) after separate contribution SUBSTANTIAL, full correctness PASS, scoped formal-support PASS, final manuscript PASS and independent portable local-release replay. The unchanged theorem includes simplex equality and strictness for every six-facet stratum. Provider failures and partial-check limits remain preserved. The full geometric theorem is not formalized; acceptance is local and publication remains stopped.

Structural r125 now has a [full author proof intake](campaigns/2026-10-14/discovery-structural-r125-parent/assessment.md) for arbitrary deterministic bounded-bias Thorp arrays, at the original full scope. It is unaccepted and earns no count. The actual T3 terminal handoff is now recovered and its detached anchors authenticate the original packet. The independent Factory Opus contribution review returns TECHNICAL, conditional on correctness; the parent has adopted that full-scope assessment with its reading limits. The separate Astra proof review now returns PASS for the entire unchanged frozen theorem relative to stipulated upstream inputs, with no repairs required. Correctness PASS and contribution TECHNICAL produce no acceptance or count. The direct Claude attempt had failed at its API rate limit without a verdict. The parent reproduced 26 artifact controls and the finite diagnostic without changing the original packet.

The campaign originally requested ten, subsequently raised to twenty, new frontier mathematics solutions, suitable for paper publication on Zenodo (interpreting “Zendoo”) and submission to mathdb.com, with supporting artifacts such as Lean formalizations. This campaign is additional to the sixteen acceptances recorded in TWENTY-FINDINGS.md at start commit `3eb9ef0f`. It does not relabel prior findings as new results or assert that the older twenty-result milestone was completed.

## Acceptance and publication

- Record the exact problem, hypotheses, quantifiers, conclusion, and remaining scope. A meaningful partial advance must be labelled as such; it cannot be advertised as solving the full named problem.
- Assume the pinned OpenAI input theorems true, at `fd4aeeb2ee4fc729c18d98444fed42fd0529eeeb`; read actual statements and companion papers. Record any newer upstream dependency separately.
- Require a complete proof, independent adversarial proof review, and separate primary-literature contribution review. Routine transfers, rediscoveries, equivalent formulations, and unproved conditional bridges do not count.
- Computational checks support proofs; they do not replace general arguments. GLM implementation and verification are reviewed independently.
- Every accepted result needs a self-contained manuscript with citations, provenance, reproducibility instructions, and a precise formalization coverage statement. Lean files must compile without `sorry` or an axiom restating the new conclusion. Explicit upstream mathematical assumptions are permitted and must be exposed. A formalized supporting lemma is not a formalized full theorem.
- Prepare publication and submission materials locally. Actual public uploads/submissions require a concrete completed package and destination/account details; no acceptance or submission is claimed before it occurs.
- Parent integrates only campaign-owned changes, and commits each accepted finding separately. Preserve unrelated and pre-existing working-tree changes.

## Model allocation

Authorized: Codex GPT-6.1-Sol/high, Pi GLM-5.3-Flash, Claude Opus 5.5, and Grok 4.7; Factory Opus 5.5 is the Claude-limit fallback. On2026-10-20 the user additionally authorized Codex/GPT alongside Factory for subagents; new Codex assignments retain standard service tier and high reasoning, with Astra for research and Sol/Astra available for reviews. Existing Factory tasks continue. Initial GLM deployment is `pi` / `spark/GLM-5.3-Flash-EXL3`, thinking high. Evaluate its actual output before broadening assignments. Independent reviewers receive the full original claim and all prior objections in each new review round.

3. GPT-6.1-Sol/high discovery: find a nonduplicative discrete/algebraic target with a substantive new bridge and tractable exact/formal support. Own outputs: `campaigns/2026-10-14/discrete-discovery/`.

Task IDs and dispositions are recorded in `campaigns/2026-10-14/tasks.json`.

## Accepted results

1. [R023 — Uniform interior gradient estimates for every finite p>=3](findings/R023-uniform-p-harmonic-gradients.md).
   Full original homogeneous claim; proof, contribution and standalone-manuscript
   gates passed. Ten supporting Lean lemmas; full PDE not formalized.
   Paper and review package prepared locally; author and open licenses set.
   Public submission and destination/account details remain outstanding.

2. [R024 — Disk rigidity for attained complete polynomial equality](findings/R024-attained-complete-polynomial-disk-rigidity.md).
   Every finite base/coefficient dimension and polynomial degree; all three
   independent gates passed. One package with the arbitrary-rank channel and
   exterior inverse, not separate counts. Reviewed 11-page paper and current 130-file
   v2 local archive verified; 33 finite exact checks, no full Lean certificate.
   Three finite scalar Lean declarations independently verified; full analytic
   theorem not formalized. Nothing publicly submitted.

3. [R025 — Functional hard-sphere fluctuations](findings/R025-functional-hard-sphere-fluctuations.md).
   Whole-space, unsmoothed deterministic grand-canonical gas on the full
   supplied regular Boltzmann interval; finite-test-vector J1 convergence and
   C-tightness. Proof, contribution and final manuscript gates passed.
   Reviewed 19-page paper, nine supporting Lean declarations and 208-file
   local archive verified. Full analytic theorem not formalized; no public submission.

4. [R026 — Integral staircase regular summand](findings/R026-integral-staircase-regular-summand.md).
   Every m >= 1, the specified integral Specht-cube orbit splits as the regular
   module, and the split maps survive every commutative-ring base change.
   Full proof, narrow contribution and manuscript gates passed; 16 supporting
   Lean theorems passed independent audit. The six-page paper and 172-file
   local archive are verified, including the full cited I21 note and exact
   coverage supplement. Full staircase combinatorics remain unformalized.
   Nothing publicly submitted.
